# Places: what one table and the place-by-place reads say of each, in the readers' words

*Made by `build_places.py` beside it; never edited by hand. AI-written (Claude Fable for the script). Each field under *read of …* is a reader's return, unchecked; what the note mended is in the note. The table's fields are from the table of 20 November; the file, the records, the dynamics and the *same statement* lines are computed by the script, as the note says. The page proves nothing, ranks nothing and holds no link. A place with no read under it was not in that read; that says nothing of the Lean. [The note](NOTE.md) says what this is and is not.*

The table's letters (20 November, [its note](../two-ways-table-2026-11-20/NOTE.md)): **a** multiplies; **b** a level that can run down; **c** gives at a cost; **d** the source is said; **e** what comes back; **f** a rule set again from a share; **g** both outcomes, boundary exact; **h** no return, or a turn. Y yes, P in part, N no.

A place is keyed by a Lean file's base name. Where several tracked files carry that name, or none does (a namespace inside a file, a program, a scratch module, a folder, a page), the row says so, since two places can then share a row; the readers' own names for the place are kept under it.

## Same statement, up to unfolding: checks filed in scratch

Read off the scratch modules filed as text in this row's study, by pattern. Each line is a check filed as scratch text, outside the package, written to close by rfl: where Lean accepted it (see the filed log on this line), the two statements are the same up to unfolding, that is, definitionally equal. The script read the text and the filed log and ran no Lean; nothing here checks that the text filed, or the file named for the other side, is the text that was built.   - laws concluding a closing: General laws: - closed_stays_closed (222). Premises: Raises (liftShare P) favours f; ReadsShare P M hA; upper; ReadsThroughPresent; NotReadmitted (∀p g, ¬present p → ¬present (f p g)); absent at r; Locked at r. Conclusion: ∀k, still absent, Locked, and share and rule not below. - lock_outlives_loss (307). Premises: Raises P favours fA; ReadsShare; upper; Accrues (p.2+1 ≤ fB p g); Locked (a,g); the course paced (join fA fB) (joinRead hA). Conclusion: loss ≥ b+t, Locked, and not below. - turned_by_loss (291). Premises: Accrues; ∀p g, N≤p.2 → ¬favours (h p g); reads r; N≤b+r. Conclusion: ¬favours at r+1. - breaks_beyond (205). Premises: Balances; GainlessOn (ds++rest) t; MetOn dep rest t; stock ds t + slack < stock ds (t+1) + lost. Conclusion: ∃c∈rest broken. - favours_breaking_beyond (293): adds apart, listed, rewards and holds. Conclusion: a broken dependence on a condition no member couples into, its formation outside the group. Built example: shapers_left (753), where the lock is left while the share falls.

  - from where an opening comes: In a balanced flow a rise is drawn from beyond, by the rise plus the loss. A rise above all the whole held needs openness (open_of_rise_above, read only as the head docstring names it). Only the part a rise's inflow covers need not be drawn from beyond.
  - a way related to widening: Yes, for narrowing and closing. A selection rewarding a rise above the slack holds only where an unseen dependence breaks. A lock read off its share stays locked while what it does not read grows, or while its readers stay gone. Nothing relates a selection to an opening.

  - what is rested on, and whose doing: A consumer draws past a family's stock (DrawsPast 1892); the family is given only by a class of feeders (FedOnlyOn 1904), each giving at most what it takes from a supply above (PassesOn 2995) or from its own store, which nothing feeds (FedFromStores 3136). The supply is again a level. The web is fixed and handed in; the laws quantify over the flows read off it; capacities are supplied or read off stores' falls.
  - from where, at what cost: Source said and cost stated, as a cost to the supply, not to the giver's standing. replenishment_le_uptake (3000): passes : PassesOn cs ds t gives replenishment cs t <= uptake ds t. passed_le_supply (3010): balances : BalancesOn W ds t, passes; stock ds (t+1) + replenishment cs t <= stock ds t + replenishment ds t. passed_le_drop (3016) adds unfed; passed_zero_of_exhausted (3023) adds stock ds t = 0. take_le_storeFall (3145). uptake_le_tiers (3069): BalancesTiers and PassesTiers at every step of the stretch; total uptake cs <= stockTiers T cs ups + total replenishment (topTier cs ups). No feeder is said to require its store. The top's source is unsaid.
  - is there a loop: Not found in what was read. Tiers run one way; the consumer is outside the class, stores outside the family, tiers apart. PassesOn and uptake_le_tiers do not ask the families to be apart, so do not forbid a loop, but no statement is of one. TwoTiers is a chain ending in a source that takes nothing.
  - general laws concluding going on: None found in what was read. Nearest: possible_iff_covers (3704), exact but of a finite stretch and of the existence of a flow. needsSome_without_end (3735), necessary: balances on cs from T, acting, only members of K give to cs, f active, present and taking at every step from T; then after every N>=T some member of K gives within the next stock N + 1 steps. needsSome_tiers (3109), one-sided. The other iffs are of need, not of going on.
  - exact two-sided law: possible_iff_covers (3704): with DecidableEq F and C, activity decided, cap : Nat -> F -> Nat supplied, distinct : Distinct cs, inside : c0 in cs, apart : Distinct ks, outside : not f in ks, alive : forall t, T<=t -> t<T+n -> W.active t f, below : W.stock cs T < n: (exists L, FedOnlyWithin W cap cs (. in ks) T n L and DrawsPast cs T n (L, W) f) <-> forall t, T<=t -> t<T+n -> W.demand cs t <= W.capacityWithout cap ks f t. Of need: needs_giver_iff_store_short (3344), premises distinct, inside, apart, outside, alive, below, own, separate, steady (no store rises in the stretch): NeedsPart (FedFromStores W store cs ks T n) (DrawsPast ...) (GivesWithin ...) f g <-> exists t in the stretch, W.capacityWithout (W.storeFall store) ks g t < W.demand cs t.
